Find the first run element\n    for run in p_elem.iter(f\"{{{NS_HWPML}}}run\"):\n        # Find the first text element in the run\n        for t in run.iter(f\"{{{NS_HWPML}}}t\"):\n            t.text = new_text\n            # Remove any other text elements in this run\n            for sibling in list(run):\n                if sibling.tag == f\"{{{NS_HWPML}}}t\" and sibling != t:\n                    run.remove(sibling)\n            break\n        # Clear other runs in the paragraph\n        for sibling in list(p_elem):\n            if sibling.tag == f\"{{{NS_HWPML}}}run\" and sibling != run:\n                p_elem.remove(sibling)\n        break\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache(p_elem):\n    \"\"\"Remove layout cache elements from a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    for child in list(p_elem):\n        if child.tag == f\"{{{NS_HWPML}}}linesegarray\":\n            p_elem.remove(child)\n\n\ndef replace_text_in_runs(p_elem, old_text, new_text):\n    \"\"\"Replace text in all runs of a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    modified = False\n    full_text = get_paragraph_text(p_elem)\n\n    if old_text in full_text:\n        new_full_text = full_text.replace(old_text, new_text)\n\n        # Mapping of old values to new values\n    replacements = {\n        \"Northwind Retail\": update_data['customer'],\n        \"이전담당자\": update_data['owner_name'],\n        \"2025-04-01 ~ 2025-04-15\": f\"{update_data['window_start']} ~ {update_data['window_end']}\",\n        \"Standard-18M\": update_data['band'],\n        \"ops-old@northwind.example\": update_data['escalation_email'],\n        \"기존 메모를 새 메모로 바꾸세요.\": update_data['note'],\n    }\n\n    # Also handle the case where customer name might be split across runs\n    # Check for \"Northwind\" and \"Retail\" separately\n    for p in root.iter(f\"{{{NS_HWPML}}}p\"):\n        para_id = p.get('id')\n        if para_id in modified_paras:\n            continue\n\n        full_text = get_paragraph_text(p)\n        if \"Northwind\" in full_text and \"Retail\" in full_text:\n            # Replace the full customer name\n            new_customer = update_data['customer']\n            set_paragraph_text(p, f\"고객사: {new_customer}\")\n            modified_paras.add(para_id)\n            remove_layout_cache(p)\n\n    tree.write(xml_path, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n    return modified_paras\n\n\ndef process_section1(xml_path, update_data, followups):\n    \"\"\"Process section1.xml with follow-up replacements and field updates.\"\"\"\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified_paras = set()\n\n    # Mapping for window date replacement\n    old_window = \"2025-04-01 ~ 2025-04-15\"\n    new_window = f\"{update_data['window_start']} ~ {update_data['window_end']}\"\n\n    # Find follow-up paragraphs and the appendix paragraph\n    followup_paras = []  # (para_id, para_element)\n    appendix_para = None\n    window_para = None\n\n    for p in root.iter(f\"{{{NS_HWPML}}}p\"):\n        para_id = p.get('id')\n        full_text = get_paragraph_text(p)\n\n        # Check for appendix sentence - preserve this\n        if \"이 부록 문단은 그대로 유지해야 합니다.\" in full_text:\n            appendix_para = p\n            continue\n\n        # Check for window confirmation line\n        if old_window in full_text:\n            window_para = p\n            continue\n\n        # Check for old follow-up patterns\n        if full_text.startswith(\"1. old follow-up\") or \\\n           full_text.startswith(\"2. old follow-up\") or \\\n           full_text.startswith(\"3. old follow-up\"):\n            followup_paras.append((para_id, p))\n\n    # Replace window dates\n    if window_para is not None:\n        para_id = window_para.get('id')\n        replace_text_in_runs(window_para, old_window, new_window)\n        modified_paras.add(para_id)\n        remove_layout_cache(window_para)\n\n    # Sort follow-up paragraphs by ID to maintain order\n    followup_paras.sort(key=lambda x: int(x[0]))\n\n    # Replace follow-up content\n    for i, (para_id, p) in enumerate(followup_paras):\n        if i < len(followups):\n            # Replace with new follow-up item\n            new_text = f\"{followups[i]['sequence']}. {followups[i]['item']}\"\n            set_paragraph_text(p, new_text)\n            modified_paras.add(para_id)\n            remove_layout_cache(p)\n\n    tree.write(xml_path, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n    return modified_paras\n\n\ndef create_hwpx(source_dir, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Create HWPX ZIP file from source directory.\"\"\"\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(source_dir):\n            for file in files:\n                file_path = os.path.join(root, file)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(file_path, source_dir)\n                zf.write(file_path, arcname)\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Paths\n    input_hwpx = '/root/renewal_playbook.hwpx'\n    output_hwpx = '/root/renewal_playbook_updated.hwpx'\n    json_path = '/root/renewal_update.json'\n    csv_path = '/root/followups.csv'\n\n    # Extract HWPX\n        extract_hwpx(input_hwpx, work_dir)\n        print(f\"Extracted to {work_dir}\")\n\n        # Process XML files\n        section0_path = os.path.join(work_dir, 'Contents', 'section0.xml')\n        section1_path = os.path.join(work_dir, 'Contents', 'section1.xml')\n\n        modified0 = process_section0(section0_path, update_data)\n        print(f\"Modified paragraphs in section0: {modified0}\")\n\n        modified1 = process_section1(section1_path, update_data, followups)\n        print(f\"Modified paragraphs in section1: {modified1}\")\n\n        #
